## Unmet requirements, by cause

### prompt_silent (364)

- **01-anti-money-laundering-pattern-detector**: The vector index dimensions match the embedding model's output. - fix: Add optional vector index creation in seed.py that reads EMBEDDING_DIMS and creates a cosmosSearch vector index, or document that vectors are not used and remove embedding-related env defaults.
- **01-anti-money-laundering-pattern-detector**: The vector index parameters are chosen for the sample's data size, and the README says how to change them for a larger one. - fix: Add a README section describing index algorithm and parameter choices for the sample data and a seed.py option to create vector indexes with adjustable params (numLists/efConstruction/M/lBuild/lSearch).
- **01-anti-money-laundering-pattern-detector**: /search returns a clear error for an empty query and when the embedding call fails. - fix: Add a /search endpoint that validates input, returns a 400 for empty queries, and catches embedding/LLM errors returning a clear 5xx/4xx error with diagnostic info.
- **01-anti-money-laundering-pattern-detector**: Search results include the similarity score. - fix: If vector search is added, include searchScore in projection and return it in API responses; update README examples.
- **01-anti-money-laundering-pattern-detector**: Unit tests run without a live cluster. - fix: Add unit tests that mock/get_db (e.g., use mongomock or monkeypatch get_db) so pytest runs offline, or provide explicit integration-test marker and document the requirement for a live DB in README.
- **01-anti-money-laundering-pattern-detector**: Infrastructure for the cluster is provided as code, using the Azure DocumentDB resource type. - fix: Add a small Bicep or Terraform example that creates Microsoft.DocumentDB/mongoClusters with minimal default settings and a 'destroy' example for cleanup.
- **01-fraud-detection-with-vector-similarity**: .env.example lists every setting with placeholder values. - fix: Add placeholders to .env.example for MODEL_NAME, EMBEDDING_DIMS (or MODEL_DIMS), INDEX_KIND, INDEX_NUM_LISTS and any other toggles (e.g., USE_LOCAL_EMBEDDINGS, ENABLE_LLM_EXPLANATIONS).
- **01-fraud-detection-with-vector-similarity**: The vector index parameters are chosen for the sample's data size, and the README says how to change them for a larger one. - fix: Add a short README section with recommended index types and parameter ranges (small demo vs medium vs large) and show the exact JSON to change in the createIndexes call.
- **01-fraud-detection-with-vector-similarity**: /search returns a clear error for an empty query and when the embedding call fails. - fix: Wrap embedding and vector_search calls with explicit exception handling and return/print distinct errors for empty input and embedding failures (catch exceptions from sentence_transformers and pymongo and provide helpful messages).
- **01-fraud-detection-with-vector-similarity**: Dependencies are pinned in requirements.txt. - fix: Pin exact compatible versions (==) or include a constraints file (pip-constraints.txt) with exact hashes to make CI reproducible.
- **01-fraud-detection-with-vector-similarity**: Unit tests run without a live cluster. - fix: Add pytest-based unit tests that mock pymongo (e.g., with mongomock or monkeypatching) to validate compute_risk, explain, and embed logic without a live cluster.
- **01-fraud-detection-with-vector-similarity**: Infrastructure for the cluster is provided as code, using the Azure DocumentDB resource type. - fix: Add a minimal IaC example (Bicep or Terraform) that creates an Azure DocumentDB mongoClusters resource (Microsoft.DocumentDB/mongoClusters) with cost-saving defaults, and reference it from the README.
- **01-patient-record-summarizer-rag**: The vector index parameters are chosen for the sample's data size, and the README says how to change them for a larger one. - fix: Add a short README section explaining why these parameter choices were made for the small (~60-doc) sample and provide recommended knobs for larger datasets (e.g. increase m/efConstruction or choose DiskANN/IVF), with copy-paste example commands.
- **01-patient-record-summarizer-rag**: Unit tests run without a live cluster. - fix: Add pytest-based unit tests that mock pymongo (or use mongomock) and mock the embedding provider so tests run offline; include instructions in README.
- **01-patient-record-summarizer-rag**: Infrastructure for the cluster is provided as code, using the Azure DocumentDB resource type. - fix: Add a minimal Bicep or Terraform (AzAPI) template that creates Microsoft.DocumentDB/mongoClusters with low-cost defaults and documents how to reuse it; or explicitly document that the sample expects an existing cluster and why IaC is omitted.
- **01-unified-customer-profile-store**: A `.env.example` lists every setting with placeholder values. - fix: Add any additional runtime env names (e.g., PORT, DB_NAME if used) to .env.example: 'PORT=3000' and 'DOCUMENTDB_URI=REPLACE_WITH_YOUR_DOCUMENTDB_URI'.
- **01-unified-customer-profile-store**: /search returns a clear error for an empty query and when the embedding call fails. - fix: If adding search, implement /search with explicit validation for an empty query and clear error paths for embedding failures (HTTP 400 for empty query, 502/503 for embedding errors).
- **01-unified-customer-profile-store**: Search results include the similarity score. - fix: If adding search, return per-result scores (e.g., similarity or distance) in the JSON response and document the score interpretation in README.
- **01-unified-customer-profile-store**: Dependencies are pinned in `requirements.txt`. - fix: Add a lockfile (package-lock.json or pnpm-lock.yaml) to the repository or pin exact versions in package.json for repeatable installs.
- **01-unified-customer-profile-store**: Infrastructure for the cluster is provided as code, using the Azure DocumentDB resource type. - fix: Add a Bicep/ARM or Terraform example that provisions Microsoft.DocumentDB/mongoClusters (or include an AVM module), parameterized for tier/region to allow reproducible infra deployment.
- **02-clinical-trial-matching-orchestration**: The vector index parameters are chosen for the sample's data size, and the README says how to change them for a larger one. - fix: Add an 'Index tuning' subsection to README that shows example cosmosSearchOptions for the chosen algorithm (DiskANN/HNSW/IVF), ties parameters (m, efConstruction, numLists, lBuild) to dataset size, and includes the exact createIndexes JSON snippet the seed script would run for production.

- **19-cross-sell-recommendation-beanie**: Infrastructure for the cluster is provided as code, using the Azure DocumentDB resource type. - fix: Add an example Bicep/ARM (or Terraform/AzAPI) snippet that uses `Microsoft.DocumentDB/mongoClusters` for deployers who want to provision a cluster; mark it as optional and warn about permissions/cost.
- **19-insider-threat-detection-pipeline**: Known anomaly profiles are held as documents in Azure DocumentDB that carry an embedding, so the pipeline can compare employee embeddings against them. - fix: Seed at least one sample anomaly_profiles document in the seed script (seed() should insert sample docs with profile_id and embedding fields) or add a small script sample_anomaly_profiles_seed() and document it in README.
- **19-insider-threat-detection-pipeline**: The vector index parameters are chosen for the sample's data size, and the README says how to change them for a larger one. - fix: Add a short 'Index tuning' section in README describing when to increase numLists/nlist/efConstruction for larger datasets and show the location in create_indexes.py to change these values; optionally expose them as env variables.
- **19-insider-threat-detection-pipeline**: A seed script loads sample data and creates the index, and can be run twice without duplicating data. - fix: Either have seed() call create_vector_index() after seeding or document in README that 'seed' and 'create_indexes' are separate steps; prefer adding a single idempotent script that calls both.
- **19-insider-threat-detection-pipeline**: "/search" returns a clear error for an empty query and when the embedding call fails. - fix: Add a minimal HTTP endpoint (Flask/FastAPI) or a documented CLI wrapper for search that validates input, returns a clear error on an empty query, and handles embedder failures (returning an informative error and a non-2xx status).
- **19-insider-threat-detection-pipeline**: Dependencies are pinned in `requirements.txt`. - fix: Pin to exact tested versions in requirements.txt (e.g., pymongo==4.20.1, openai==3.59.0, python-dotenv==1.2.4, pytest==9.2.0, numpy==1.26.x) and document why these versions were selected.
- **19-insider-threat-detection-pipeline**: Infrastructure for the cluster is provided as code, using the Azure DocumentDB resource type. - fix: Add a minimal IaC example (Bicep or Terraform) that declares a Microsoft.DocumentDB/mongoClusters resource with parameters (tier, storage, region) and default low-cost values, and document how to apply it.
- **19-insider-threat-detection-pipeline**: The README covers what the sample shows, prerequisites, setup, run, and clean-up. - fix: Add a 'Clean-up' section with commands to drop created collections (activity_logs, employee_vectors, alerts, anomaly_profiles) and, if IaC is supplied, how to delete the created cluster or resource group.
- **19-option-chain-data-store**: `/search` returns a clear error for an empty query and when the embedding call fails. - fix: Add a /search endpoint with input validation that returns 400 for empty query and explicit error handling for embedding failures (example: return JSON {error:'embedding_failed'} with 502) and tests that simulate embedding failure.
- **19-option-chain-data-store**: Search results include the similarity score. - fix: If a /search endpoint is added, include similarity scores in response objects (e.g., include 'score' per hit) and test coverage for score presence.
- **19-option-chain-data-store**: Infrastructure for the cluster is provided as code, using the Azure DocumentDB resource type. - fix: Provide an IaC example (Bicep or Terraform AzAPI) targeting Microsoft.DocumentDB/mongoClusters and include it under infra/ with sensible defaults and clean-up instructions.
- **19-proactive-outreach-orchestration**: /search returns a clear error for an empty query and when the embedding call fails. - fix: Add a /search route that validates input (400 on empty query), calls the embedding model in a try/catch and returns a 502 or 500 with a clear error message when embedding fails.
- **19-proactive-outreach-orchestration**: Search results include the similarity score. - fix: If adding vector search, return each result with its similarity score in the /search JSON payload (score field), and document the metric semantics in README.
- **19-proactive-outreach-orchestration**: Unit tests run without a live cluster. - fix: Add unit tests that mock the MongoClient (or use mongodb-memory-server) and a test that stubs the OpenAI client so CI tests can run without a live cluster or API keys.
- **19-proactive-outreach-orchestration**: Infrastructure for the cluster is provided as code, using the Azure DocumentDB resource type. - fix: Add a small Bicep or Terraform example that creates a Microsoft.DocumentDB/mongoClusters resource with parameterized tier, storage and region; keep defaults low-cost for gallery use.
- **20-identity-resolution-multi-agent**: The vector index parameters are chosen for the sample's data size, and the README says how to change them for a larger one. - fix: Add a short 'Index tuning' section to README explaining the chosen HNSW params, when to switch to DiskANN, and how to change dimensions and lBuild/maxDegree for larger datasets (reference documentdb-vector-search guidance).
- **20-identity-resolution-multi-agent**: Dependencies are pinned in requirements.txt. - fix: Pin exact versions or provide a constraints.txt. Replace '>=' specs with '==' pins for a reproducible sample and CI (e.g., beanie==2.2.0).
- **20-identity-resolution-multi-agent**: Infrastructure for the cluster is provided as code, using the Azure DocumentDB resource type. - fix: Add a small Bicep or Terraform snippet using Microsoft.DocumentDB/mongoClusters (or link to the portal CLI snippet) as an optional 'provisioning' artifact. Mark it optional in README and gate actual provisioning behind an operator/az CLI step.
- **20-identity-resolution-multi-agent**: The README covers what the sample shows, prerequisites, setup, run, and clean-up. - fix: Add a 'Clean-up' subsection to README with steps to delete created collections or use a provided helper script; add an 'Index tuning' note as recommended above.
- **20-interest-accrual-batch-processor**: A .env.example lists every setting with placeholder values. - fix: Add SEED_COUNT (and any other optional env vars used by scripts) to .env.example with a placeholder and short comment.
- **20-interest-accrual-batch-processor**: A seed script loads sample data and creates the index, and can be run twice without duplicating data. - fix: Either have the seed script also call ensureIndexes (or a separate idempotent migration script), or document that indexes are created at startup and provide a separate idempotent 'migrate' script for deployment-time index creation.
- **20-interest-accrual-batch-processor**: Infrastructure for the cluster is provided as code, using the Azure DocumentDB resource type. - fix: Add an IaC example (Bicep/ARM or Terraform) that uses Microsoft.DocumentDB/mongoClusters with parameters for tier, region, and storage, or add a note explaining why provisioning is out of scope.
- **20-open-banking-api-cache-layer**: A seed script loads sample data and creates the index, and can be run twice without duplicating data. - fix: Add an explicit call in src/seed.js (or startup) to ensure the model's indexes are created, e.g. await ApiCache.createIndexes(); before inserting data. This ensures index creation irrespective of Mongoose autoIndex defaults.
- **20-open-banking-api-cache-layer**: Dependencies are pinned in `requirements.txt`. - fix: Either (a) add a repository file documenting exact dependency versions (e.g. lockfile or a dependencies list), or (b) replace caret ranges in package.json with exact versions (e.g. "mongoose": "7.5.0"), and commit package-lock.json. For gallery policy, add a short README line that the lockfile must be committed and list exact versions.
- **20-open-banking-api-cache-layer**: Unit tests run without a live cluster. - fix: Add minimal unit tests using mongodb-memory-server or by mocking Mongoose. For example, add a simple Jest test that launches an in-memory MongoDB and exercises computeKey/getOrUpdate paths with a mock fetcher.
- **20-open-banking-api-cache-layer**: Infrastructure for the cluster is provided as code, using the Azure DocumentDB resource type. - fix: Add an optional Bicep/Terraform example that shows Microsoft.DocumentDB/mongoClusters (or a referenced AzAPI module) to create a DocumentDB cluster. Keep it optional and documented in the README.
- **20-provider-directory-semantic-search**: The vector index parameters are chosen for the sample's data size, and the README says how to change them for a larger one. - fix: Add a short 'Index tuning' section to README describing when to increase m/efConstruction, when to choose DiskANN/IVF, and where to change constants in seed.py; provide recommended presets for demo/small/production.
- **20-provider-directory-semantic-search**: /search returns a clear error for an empty query and when the embedding call fails. - fix: Wrap the embedding call in try/except and return a 502 (or 500) with a readable message when external embedding fails; include retries/timeout where appropriate.
- **20-provider-directory-semantic-search**: Infrastructure for the cluster is provided as code, using the Azure DocumentDB resource type. - fix: Add a minimal IaC template (Bicep or Terraform) that uses Microsoft.DocumentDB/mongoClusters with parameters for tier, region and storage; include a 'deploy-if-desired' section in README and a caution not to run it in user accounts without approval.
- **20-provider-directory-semantic-search**: The README covers what the sample shows, prerequisites, setup, run, and clean-up. - fix: Add a 'Clean-up' subsection to README showing how to drop the seeded collection or database (mongo client command or az CLI snippet) and how to remove any created local .env files.
- **20-store-locator-with-geospatial-search**: The vector index dimensions match the embedding model's output. - fix: If the sample will include vector search, add an explicit step to the starter prompt (or skill) to create vector indexes with dimensions matching the chosen embedding model and add those parameters to the seed/migration script and README. Prefer adding a rule to the documentdb-vector-search skill or a README section saying exactly which embedding model and dimension the sample uses.
- **20-store-locator-with-geospatial-search**: The vector index parameters are chosen for the sample's data size, and the README says how to change them for a larger one. - fix: If vector search is added, include an explicit README section describing index engine choice (DiskANN/HNSW/IVF), parameters (lBuild/lSearch/maxDegree) for small sample vs larger datasets, and an example createIndexes JSON.
- **20-store-locator-with-geospatial-search**: /search returns a clear error for an empty query and when the embedding call fails. - fix: If a /search endpoint is required, add it to the starter prompt and implement explicit validation (400 for empty query) and error handling around embedding calls (return 502/503 with a clear error on embedding failures).
- **20-store-locator-with-geospatial-search**: Search results include the similarity score. - fix: If adding semantic/vector search, include similarity score in the aggregation pipeline response (e.g., distance/score field), and document it in README and API responses.
- **20-store-locator-with-geospatial-search**: Dependencies are pinned in requirements.txt. - fix: Either (A) change the checklist to accept package-lock.json for Node projects, or (B) pin dependency versions precisely in package.json (no caret) and include package-lock.json. Smallest immediate fix for this repo: commit package-lock.json (already present) and add a README note that package-lock.json pins the dependencies.
- **20-store-locator-with-geospatial-search**: Unit tests run without a live cluster. - fix: Add unit tests that use an in-memory MongoDB (mongodb-memory-server) or mocks, and document how to run them without a live cluster in README.
- **20-store-locator-with-geospatial-search**: Infrastructure for the cluster is provided as code, using the Azure DocumentDB resource type. - fix: Add an IaC template (Bicep or Terraform) that creates Microsoft.DocumentDB/mongoClusters with parameters for tier, storage and region, or provide a short example in README linking to the official quickstart template.
- **retail-semantic-search**: The app is a Python Flask app. - fix: Align the reference with the explicit Node.js prompt. If Python is intended instead, replace 'Node.js LlamaIndex agent' with 'Python Flask app'; do not ask a skill to override the requested runtime.
- **retail-semantic-search**: Product titles and descriptions are embedded with text-embedding-ada-002. - fix: If this model choice is intentional, add 'Use text-embedding-ada-002' to the prompt. Otherwise update the reference to the documented text-embedding-3-small example; the vector skill requires consistency, not ada-002.
- **retail-semantic-search**: Embeddings are stored as BSON binary vectors. - fix: Correct the reference to the documented numeric-array representation. The fetched Azure DocumentDB vector page requires number[]; do not add a binary-storage rule without service-specific support documentation.
- **retail-semantic-search**: A vector-ivf index exists on the embedding field. - fix: Align the reference with DiskANN, or explicitly replace DiskANN with IVF in the prompt if that is the desired sample.
- **retail-semantic-search**: /search takes a natural language query, embeds it, and returns the 10 most similar products. - fix: Align the reference with the product-ID CLI, or explicitly request an HTTP /search route accepting natural-language input and returning ten results.
- **retail-semantic-search**: /search returns a clear error for an empty query and when the embedding call fails. - fix: If the HTTP sample is intended, explicitly request /search and its empty-input/provider-error contract. Otherwise change the reference to the product-ID and ingestion interfaces actually requested.
- **retail-semantic-search**: Dependencies are pinned in requirements.txt. - fix: Make the reference runtime-neutral: 'Dependencies are exact-pinned in the runtime's manifest and lockfile.' Require requirements.txt only for a Python prompt.
- **retail-semantic-search**: Infrastructure for the cluster is provided as code, using the Azure DocumentDB resource type. - fix: Add a gallery-packaging skill rule: include optional, nonexecuted parameterized Microsoft.DocumentDB/mongoClusters infrastructure, or explicitly document an approved existing-cluster-only exception. Deployment skills teach how to provision but do not require artifacts for every app.
- **retail-semantic-search**: The sample uses the name Azure DocumentDB throughout, and no former or neighbouring product name. - fix: Clarify the gallery naming rule: require correct identification and permit explicit disambiguation and unchanged API names. If literal exclusion is intended, state that separately; it is stricter than the public naming guidance.

### builder_error (242)

- **01-anti-money-laundering-pattern-detector**: Implement the agent in Python with LangChain as a single agent that is given a DocumentDB aggregation tool and invokes that tool to analyse transactions. - fix: Wrap the aggregation functions as a LangChain Tool and create a simple LangChain Agent that calls that tool (or update run.py to instantiate a LangChain Agent with a Tool that calls aml_agent.analysis).
- **01-anti-money-laundering-pattern-detector**: Dependencies are pinned in `requirements.txt`. - fix: Pin to exact tested package versions (e.g., pymongo==4.20.1, langchain==1.5.2, openai==3.59.0, python-dotenv==1.2.4 etc.) or add constraints file with exact versions.
- **01-fraud-detection-with-vector-similarity**: A Python LangChain agent is the single entry point: it accepts a new transaction and runs the fraud check for it. - fix: Wrap the existing vector_search/compute_risk/explain functions in a LangChain Tool and provide a minimal LangChain Agent instantiation in run_agent.py (or add a separate agent.py that registers the tool and exposes the same CLI).
- **01-fraud-detection-with-vector-similarity**: A cosmosSearch index of kind vector-ivf is created in code on that embedding field, with dimensions matching the embedding model's output (similarity e.g. COS). - fix: Add idempotent index-creation code (e.g., a create_index() in seed.py that issues the createIndexes command with cosmosSearchOptions including kind:'vector-ivf' and dimensions=len(vec)) so the sample can create/verify the index from code.
- **01-fraud-detection-with-vector-similarity**: A LangChain tool performs the vector search against Azure DocumentDB with a $search / cosmosSearch query using the new transaction's embedding as the query vector, and returns the similar historical cases. - fix: Wrap vector_search as a LangChain Tool (or Retriever) and register it in the sample agent; add an example of calling the tool from a LangChain Agent in the CLI.
- **01-fraud-detection-with-vector-similarity**: On each new transaction the agent invokes that vector search tool and derives a fraud risk score from the returned similar cases (e.g. based on how closely they match). - fix: Have the LangChain Agent call the registered LangChain Tool that performs the vector search, or document that the CLI is a minimal agent implementation; prefer adding the LangChain Tool wrapper so the sample matches the starter prompt.
- **01-fraud-detection-with-vector-similarity**: A seed script loads sample data and creates the index, and can be run twice without duplicating data. - fix: Add idempotent index creation (createIndexes) to seed.py before upserts so seed.py fully prepares the collection and can be re-run safely.
- **01-patient-record-summarizer-rag**: A Python RAG agent built with LlamaIndex answers a clinical question about a patient: one agent retrieves relevant record sections from Azure DocumentDB and produces the summary from them. - fix: Add and run a short script (e.g. scripts/run_llama_rag.py) that constructs a LlamaIndex VectorStoreIndex for the collection, ingests a small sample, and runs a query to produce a prompt-engineered summary; call it from the README so it is executed in verification.
- **01-patient-record-summarizer-rag**: The summarization prompt is assembled deliberately (context engineering): instructions for a clinical summary, the retrieved sections as context and the clinical question are placed in a structured layout. - fix: Add a function assemble_clinical_prompt(sections, question, instructions) and call the chosen LLM (or LlamaIndex query engine) with that structured prompt; include the example prompt template in README.
- **01-patient-record-summarizer-rag**: A seed script loads sample data and creates the index, and can be run twice without duplicating data. - fix: Either have the seed script call create_vector_index.py after seeding (guarded by existence-check) or document in README that users should run create_vector_index.py after seeding; prefer making seed idempotent and create the index if absent.
- **01-patient-record-summarizer-rag**: /search returns a clear error for an empty query and when the embedding call fails. - fix: Add explicit validation at the start of the /search handler (return 400 for empty question) and wrap the embedding call in try/except returning 502 or 500 with a clear error message on embedding failure.
- **01-patient-record-summarizer-rag**: Dependencies are pinned in requirements.txt. - fix: Pin to specific versions (e.g. pymongo==4.20.1, fastapi==0.95.x) in requirements.txt or provide a constraints file; include the environment/venv used during verification.
- **01-patient-record-summarizer-rag**: The README covers what the sample shows, prerequisites, setup, run, and clean-up. - fix: Add a 'Clean up' section describing how to drop the sample collection and any resource cleanup steps (e.g., drop database or delete cluster if provisioned via IaC).
- **02-ai-powered-product-recommendations**: Generate vector embeddings for products from the co-purchase data (code contains a clearly defined step that creates embeddings per product from the mined co-purchase information). - fix: In src/seed.js, compute a co-purchase summary or neighborhood for each product (e.g., top co-purchased product IDs or co-purchase counts) and pass that aggregated textual/numeric representation into getEmbeddingForText so embeddings reflect co-purchase relationships rather than only product title.
- **02-clinical-trial-matching-orchestration**: A Python pipeline built with LangChain acts as the orchestrator: it accepts a patient profile as input and runs the steps in order, vector search, then the eligibility agent, then the consent agent, passing each step's output to the next. - fix: Use LangChain Chains/Agents to wrap the eligibility and consent steps (e.g., create two simple Chains or Agents that accept the candidate list and patient profile) and call them from rank_and_match; or document that the LangChain ChatModel is used and rename 'agent' to 'LLM step' if Chain/Agent usage is not desired.
- **02-clinical-trial-matching-orchestration**: An eligibility agent, built with LangChain, receives the candidate trials and applies each trial's inclusion and exclusion rules to the patient profile to decide which candidates the patient is eligible for. - fix: Wrap deterministic_eligibility_check in a LangChain Chain or Agent (or implement a simple LangChain Tool that executes the deterministic rules) so the project uses LangChain for all agent/chain steps as requested by the starter prompt.
- **02-real-time-event-stream-ingestion**: A `.env.example` lists every setting with placeholder values. - fix: Add optional and recommended env variables to .env.example (e.g., MAX_POOL_SIZE, MAX_IDLE_TIME_MS, SERVER_SELECTION_TIMEOUT_MS, LOG_LEVEL, and any auth-mode toggle) with placeholder values and short comments.
- **02-real-time-event-stream-ingestion**: The README covers what the sample shows, prerequisites, setup, run, and clean-up. - fix: Add a 'Clean-up' section to README that shows commands to drop the events collection or remove seed data and instructions to delete any test artifacts.
- **02-swift-message-parser-store**: Each parsed message is checked for its required fields before storage; missing required fields are not written. - fix: Make amount/currency/value_date required in the Pydantic/Beanie model or add an explicit check in /ingest to skip/return an error for messages missing required fields (e.g. if any of amount/currency/value_date is None: record validation error and do not include in bulk insert).
- **02-swift-message-parser-store**: .env.example lists every setting with placeholder values. - fix: Extend .env.example to list all runtime-configurable settings used or intended (e.g. DOCUMENTDB_URI, DATABASE_NAME, LOG_LEVEL, BATCH_SIZE, TIMEOUT_MS), each with a placeholder and short comment.
- **02-swift-message-parser-store**: Dependencies are pinned in requirements.txt. - fix: Pin tested exact package versions in requirements.txt (e.g. beanie==1.22.1 motor==3.1.1 pymongo==4.4.0) or include a pip-tools/constraints file and document the tested matrix in README.
- **02-transaction-categorization-agent**: The agent reaches Azure DocumentDB through LangChain tools (e.g. one tool that fetches uncategorised transactions and one that writes a category back), and the agent decides when to invoke them. - fix: Implement DB access as LangChain Tools (e.g. fetch_uncategorized, write_category) and register them in the LangChain agent; or change the prompt requirement to accept direct DB calls.
- **03-next-best-action-multi-agent**: The system is written in Python and its agents and orchestrator are built with LangChain (LangChain Python), with the propensity agent, channel agent and timing agent each a separate agent that the orchestrator invokes. - fix: Wrap the existing functions in lightweight LangChain agent wrappers (or implement LangChain Tools/Agents that call these functions) and update orchestrator to instantiate/use LangChain Agent instances when OPENAI_API_KEY is present.
- **03-next-best-action-multi-agent**: The vector index parameters are chosen for the sample's data size, and the README says how to change them for a larger one. - fix: Add a README section describing a recommended vector index definition (kind, dimensions, similarity, maxDegree, lBuild, lSearch guidance) and show how to change those parameters when scaling (with example commands).
- **03-next-best-action-multi-agent**: Dependencies are pinned in requirements.txt. - fix: Pin dependencies to exact tested versions (e.g., pymongo==4.5.0, python-dotenv==1.0.0, fastapi==0.95.0, etc.) and record them in requirements.txt / constraints.txt.
- **03-next-best-action-multi-agent**: The README covers what the sample shows, prerequisites, setup, run, and clean-up. - fix: Add a 'Clean-up' section that shows how to remove created collections or drop the sample database (e.g. mongo shell commands or a provided cleanup.py script).
- **03-substitute-product-finder**: A script ingests 10,000 product transactions into Azure DocumentDB through the Beanie model. - fix: Either (a) insert via Product(**doc).insert() in batches using Beanie helpers, or (b) document that the insert_many path is intentional. If Beanie usage is required by policy, change insertion to use Beanie bulk helpers or upsert semantics.
- **03-substitute-product-finder**: .env.example lists every setting with placeholder values. - fix: Add DB_NAME and any other optional settings to .env.example with placeholders and short comments (e.g., DB_NAME=sample_retail_03).
- **03-substitute-product-finder**: A seed script loads sample data and creates the index, and can be run twice without duplicating data. - fix: Make inserts idempotent by using upsert semantics keyed on product_id (e.g., replace_one({'product_id': pid}, doc, upsert=True) in batches) or have the script check for and skip existing product_ids.
- **04-customer-journey-mapper**: Build the application in Node.js as a single LlamaIndex agent that owns the whole flow: it invokes the aggregation tool, formats the result into its prompt and produces the final summary. - fix: Have the agent module import and call getCustomerJourney(customerId) (or expose a run(customerId) API) so the agent both invokes the aggregation tool and performs prompt construction + summarization in one place.
- **04-customer-journey-mapper**: The agent takes the customer whose journey is to be mapped as its input (e.g. a customer identifier) and passes it to the aggregation tool. - fix: Change agent.summarizeJourney to accept customerId, import getCustomerJourney, and call it internally, or add an agent.run(customerId) wrapper that performs both fetching and summarization.
- **04-customer-journey-mapper**: Register a DocumentDB aggregation tool with the LlamaIndex agent that runs an aggregation pipeline (e.g. with the `mongodb` Node.js driver) over the touchpoint event documents stored in Azure DocumentDB for that customer. - fix: Implement the aggregation as a server-side pipeline (coll.aggregate([...]) ), and either export the aggregation function as a tool the agent imports or register it using the agent-tooling convention you adopt (one-line change: replace find().sort().toArray() with an aggregate pipeline).
- **04-customer-journey-mapper**: The pipeline itself reconstructs the journey: it sorts the customer's events by timestamp and groups them by channel (e.g. a `$match` on the customer, a `$sort` on the timestamp and a `$group` by channel). - fix: Use a pipeline like [{$match:{customerId}}, {$sort:{timestamp:1}}, {$group:{_id:'$channel', events:{$push:'$$ROOT'}}}] and return the pipeline result (or both timeline and grouped aggregates) instead of performing grouping client-side.
- **04-earnings-call-rag-assistant**: Build a Python RAG pipeline with LlamaIndex that ingests earnings call transcripts from PDF files and extracts their text. - fix: Add PDF->text conversion (pdfminer.six or PyPDF2) and integrate LlamaIndex's ingestion APIs (e.g., SimpleDirectoryReader / PDFReader and LlamaIndex index creation) in src/ingest.py.
- **04-earnings-call-rag-assistant**: Split each transcript into chunks with LlamaIndex, keeping on every chunk metadata that identifies its source transcript and the timestamp of the passage. - fix: Parse timestamp tokens (e.g. lines like '[00:15]') during chunking and populate start_time/end_time for each chunk; keep deterministic chunk_id scheme already present (sha256 of source:start:end).
- **04-earnings-call-rag-assistant**: Embed each chunk with the embedding model configured in LlamaIndex and store each chunk in Azure DocumentDB as a document holding the chunk text, its embedding array and its source and timestamp metadata. - fix: Either compute embeddings via LlamaIndex's embedding wrapper or explicitly document that the sample uses sentence-transformers; if the gallery mandates LlamaIndex, replace model.encode calls with LlamaIndex embedding calls and/or show LlamaIndex vector-store integration.
- **04-earnings-call-rag-assistant**: Apply context engineering before generation: select and trim the retrieved passages so that their combined size fits within the LLM's context window (e.g. by token counting), then assemble the prompt from those passages. - fix: Implement token-budget-aware assembly: estimate tokens (tiktoken or approximate char→token ratio), reserve answer buffer, then select/truncate passages by score until remaining budget is exhausted.
- **04-earnings-call-rag-assistant**: Generate the answer with the LLM through LlamaIndex from the assembled context of retrieved passages. - fix: Integrate LlamaIndex LLMPredictor/ServiceContext and call its query/summarize APIs against the assembled context, or clearly document why direct OpenAI calls are used.
- **04-earnings-call-rag-assistant**: Include in each answer a citation of the source transcript and timestamp of the passages the answer draws on. - fix: Extract timestamps from transcripts during chunking and store them in start_time/end_time so the citations printed by the synthesizer are accurate.
- **04-earnings-call-rag-assistant**: A seed script loads sample data and creates the index, and can be run twice without duplicating data. - fix: Make the ingest idempotent without raising by using upsert semantics (bulk replace_one upsert=True) or check-for-existence per chunk before insert; handle DuplicateKeyError explicitly for a silent re-run.
- **04-medical-imaging-metadata-store**: For each imaging record, the findings text is embedded with OpenAI and the resulting vector is stored on the same document as a BSON binary vector. - fix: If the intent is to follow the original prompt literally, change seed/main to pack embeddings into bson.Binary. If the intent is to use native DocumentDB vector indexes, update the starter prompt to say "store embeddings as number[] suitable for DocumentDB vector indexes". Prefer the latter (prompt change).
- **04-natural-language-product-search**: Dependencies are pinned in requirements.txt. - fix: Pin exact tested versions in requirements.txt (e.g., fastapi==0.95.0 uvicorn==0.22.0 pymongo==4.20.1 langchain==0.0.XXX openai==... python-dotenv==...).
- **04-regulatory-reporting-orchestration**: The pipeline is written in Python and built with LangChain, with an orchestrator and three distinct agent roles: data extraction, validation and filing. - fix: Either wire LangChain Agent/Chain objects into the orchestrator (add langchain to requirements and replace or wrap the simple agent classes with LangChain constructs) or update the README and prompt to say the sample demonstrates a LangChain-style orchestration using plain Python wrappers.
- **04-regulatory-reporting-orchestration**: A .env.example lists every setting with placeholder values. - fix: Align variable names: add MODEL_NAME to .env.example (or change agents.py to read CHAT_MODEL).

- **04-regulatory-reporting-orchestration**: The README covers what the sample shows, prerequisites, setup, run, and clean-up. - fix: Add a 'clean-up' section to README describing how to remove seeded documents/collections or a script (e.g. scripts/teardown.py) to drop the sample collections.
- **05-appointment-scheduling-assistant**: The agent has agent memory that carries the conversation context across turns, so a follow-up such as "move it an hour later" is resolved against the appointment discussed earlier without the patient restating the provider, date or time. - fix: Implement explicit relative-time parsing/rewriting step: after the model requests a reschedule with a relative expression, look up lastAppointment in memory, compute the target ISO time, call findAvailableSlots with that target time, and then call rescheduleAppointmentAtomic. Add a helper in agent.js that converts relative phrases to a Date using the saved appointment start time.
- **05-appointment-scheduling-assistant**: When a patient gives a relative time such as "an hour later", the agent computes the target time from the remembered appointment time and uses the availability tool to find a matching slot in Azure DocumentDB before rescheduling. - fix: Add a relative-time resolution step in agent.handleMessage that: (a) reads the last appointment from the user's BufferMemory, (b) parses the relative expression (e.g., 'an hour later') to compute a target ISO datetime, (c) calls tools.findAvailableSlots(providerId, targetISO, targetISO) to find a slot, then calls tools.rescheduleAppointment with that slotId.

- **17-rare-disease-literature-search**: Generate an embedding for each chunk with LlamaIndex's embedding integration and store the chunks with their embedding vectors as fields on documents in Azure DocumentDB (through LlamaIndex's Azure DocumentDB vector store integration or a MongoDB-compatible Python driver such as PyMongo). - fix: Either use LlamaIndex's OpenAIEmbedding (or LlamaIndex vector-store adapter) in embed_texts, or document in README why direct OpenAI calls were used; change the code to call LlamaIndex's embedding API when you want a full LlamaIndex integration.
- **17-rare-disease-literature-search**: Expose a RAG agent built with LlamaIndex in Python that takes a researcher question, retrieves the semantically ranked chunks from Azure DocumentDB and generates an answer conditioned on those chunks. - fix: Either (A) wire the retrieval step + prompt/LLM call into a LlamaIndex ServiceContext/LLMPredictor + Retriever pipeline, or (B) change sample description/README to state that retrieval is via direct cosmosSearch+ChatCompletion rather than a LlamaIndex-managed RAG object.
- **17-rare-disease-literature-search**: Dependencies are pinned in requirements.txt. - fix: Pin critical dependencies to exact tested versions (e.g., 'llama-index==0.14.26', 'pymongo==4.20.1', 'openai==3.59.0') in requirements.txt or provide a constraints file; document why pins were chosen.
- **17-rare-disease-literature-search**: The README covers what the sample shows, prerequisites, setup, run, and clean-up. - fix: Add a 'clean-up' section to README.md with commands to drop the collection and guidance on how to delete the sample DB or the cluster if the user provisioned one.
- **17-seasonal-trend-spotter-agent**: The aggregation tool runs an aggregation pipeline against Azure DocumentDB through a MongoDB driver and the pipeline's stages compute sales velocity per season and compare it across seasons. - fix: Add pipeline stages (e.g., $group + $setWindowFields or $group + $lookup + $project) to compute season-to-season change/delta per category and return the derived change field.
- **17-seasonal-trend-spotter-agent**: A context-engineering step shapes the two tool outputs into the prompt sent to the model, combining the sales-velocity comparison and the web trend signals into one prompt context. - fix: Add a prompt template and a function that formats DB and web signals into labelled sections (e.g., 'DB signals' / 'Web signals') and pass that as context to the LLM call (LangChain chat/call).
- **17-seasonal-trend-spotter-agent**: The agent's final answer is a ranked list of product categories that identifies which are rising and which are declining (ranked by season-to-season change). - fix: Change the DB aggregation to return per-category season totals and compute season-to-season delta per category in code or in the pipeline, then rank by magnitude of the delta and label rising/declining.
- **17-seasonal-trend-spotter-agent**: Dependencies are pinned in requirements.txt. - fix: Pin exact versions (e.g. pymongo==4.19.0, langchain==1.5.2, openai==3.59.0) or provide a constraints.txt for reproducible installs.
- **17-stress-testing-multi-agent-runner**: Unit tests run without a live cluster. - fix: Change the test's fake_db.__getitem__.side_effect to return a stable MagicMock for 'stress_runs' (pre-create fake_stress_runs = MagicMock() and return it), or use mongomock to simulate the DB.
- **18-360-data-platform-cdp-orchestration**: Ingest, dedup and enrich are distinct LangChain-based components the orchestrator invokes. - fix: Wrap the agent classes as LangChain Runnables/Chains/Agents (e.g., subclass Runnable or use Chains) and call those objects from the orchestrator, or document why simple classes are sufficient.
- **18-adverse-event-reporting-pipeline**: A seed script loads sample data and creates the index, and can be run twice without duplicating data. - fix: Change the seed to upsert or make it idempotent, e.g. create a suitable unique index and use update_one(filter, {'$setOnInsert': doc}, upsert=True) for each sample or run bulk upsert.
- **18-adverse-event-reporting-pipeline**: Dependencies are pinned in requirements.txt. - fix: Pin exact versions for all runtime/test dependencies (replace ranges with exact versions) to improve reproducibility.
- **18-adverse-event-reporting-pipeline**: Unit tests run without a live cluster. - fix: Add unit tests that mock the DB (e.g., using mongomock or pytest fixtures) so they run offline; mark integration tests that require DOCUMENTDB_URI separately.
- **18-sanctions-list-screening-agent**: Implement the screening agent in Python with LangChain as a single agent that takes a screening request and carries it through searching, consolidation, scoring and the report. - fix: Add minimal LangChain integration (e.g., instantiate a LangChain RetrievalQA or Agent that uses a DocumentDB retriever and call it from the /screen handler) or update code comments/README to explain why LangChain is omitted; prefer adding a small wrapper that uses LangChain's retriever API.
- **18-sanctions-list-screening-agent**: A seed script loads sample data and creates the index, and can be run twice without duplicating data. - fix: Make the seed script optionally create the vector index idempotently (call createIndexes with handling for 'index exists') or provide a single idempotent 'bootstrap' script that seeds and creates/verifies the index.
- **18-sanctions-list-screening-agent**: /search returns a clear error for an empty query and when the embedding call fails. - fix: Wrap the openai.Embedding.create call with try/except and raise an HTTPException with a clear error message explaining embedding failure and how to remediate (e.g., check OPENAI_API_KEY, network).
- **19-care-coordination-multi-agent**: A .env.example lists every setting with placeholder values. - fix: Add EMBEDDING_DIM to .env.example (e.g., EMBEDDING_DIM="1536") and mention which values are required when enabling embeddings/vector indexes.
- **19-cross-sell-recommendation-beanie**: A Python aggregation step, run through Beanie ODM's aggregation support over the order-history documents in Azure DocumentDB, derives co-purchase product pairs and persists one document per pair back to Azure DocumentDB through Beanie. - fix: Use a Beanie/Motor aggregation pipeline (collection.aggregate or Beanie's aggregation helpers) to compute pair counts server-side, then upsert each pair document. Concretely: replace the in-Python loop over orders with an aggregation that $unwinds and $group to produce pair counts, then bulk upsert results into product_pairs.
- **19-cross-sell-recommendation-beanie**: Handling a /cross-sell request embeds the basket with the same OpenAI embedding call used for the pairs and runs a $search / cosmosSearch aggregation against the vector index, so the similarity between the basket and the stored pair vectors is computed inside Azure DocumentDB. - fix: Modify the endpoint to call the embeddings API at request time (or explicitly document why stored product embeddings are equivalent) and run a server-side aggregation using `$search` with `cosmosSearch` vector query (use motor's aggregate/runCommand to call createSearchIndexes/$search), passing the basket vector so the DB performs nearest-neighbor retrieval.
- **19-cross-sell-recommendation-beanie**: The service is asynchronous end to end: request handling, every Beanie ODM call to Azure DocumentDB (aggregation, writes, index creation and the $search query) and every OpenAI embedding call use Python async/await. - fix: Implement async server-side index creation and $search calls (motor runCommand/aggregate) and, if performing on-demand embeddings, call OpenAI via an async client in the request path.
- **19-cross-sell-recommendation-beanie**: The vector index parameters are chosen for the sample's data size, and the README says how to change them for a larger one. - fix: Add `index_example.json` with recommended parameters for small/medium/large seeds (IVF/HNSW/DiskANN choices and lBuild/lSearch/ef parameters) and a README subsection showing which knobs to change with dataset-scale guidance.
- **19-cross-sell-recommendation-beanie**: A seed script loads sample data and creates the index, and can be run twice without duplicating data. - fix: Make seed idempotent: use deterministic IDs (no random suffix) or upsert orders instead of inserting new ones, and use bulk upsert for product_pairs. Add an idempotent index-creation step separate from data load.
- **19-cross-sell-recommendation-beanie**: /search returns a clear error for an empty query and when the embedding call fails. - fix: If request-time embeddings are used, add try/except around the OpenAI call and return a 502/503 with a clear message on embedding failure (with appropriate retries/timeouts).
- **19-cross-sell-recommendation-beanie**: The README covers what the sample shows, prerequisites, setup, run, and clean-up. - fix: Add a 'clean-up' section to README showing how to drop the sample database/collections or commands to delete sample resources (eg. mongo shell dropDatabase or use motor to drop collections).
- **19-insider-threat-detection-pipeline**: A Python pipeline reads employee activity logs from Azure DocumentDB and aggregates them into one behavioural feature vector per employee using an Azure DocumentDB aggregation pipeline. - fix: Replace the client-side grouping with a server-side aggregation pipeline (logs.aggregate([...]) using $match (timestamp >= start) then $group by employee_id to compute counts and averages) and use the aggregated result in ingest_once.
- **19-insider-threat-detection-pipeline**: The job compares each employee's recent activity embedding against the known anomaly profiles with a $search / cosmosSearch query against the vector-ivf index. - fix: Use a $search aggregation with cosmosSearch to query the anomaly_profiles collection (or query the anomaly_profiles collection by running an aggregation with $search: {cosmosSearch:{vector: recent, path:'embedding', k: N}}) and project {$meta:'searchScore'} to obtain the engine score instead of local comparisons.
- **19-proactive-outreach-orchestration**: An orchestrator built with LangChain in Node.js coordinates the three agents, handing each qualifying event from the trigger agent to the copy agent and the copy agent's output to the send agent, in that order. - fix: Replace the simple function calls with a minimal LangChain Chain/LLMChain composition (e.g., construct an LLMChain that calls the LLM prompt template for the copy agent and a separate chain/tool wrapper for the send agent) so the sample demonstrates LangChain orchestration explicitly.
- **19-proactive-outreach-orchestration**: A copy agent generates personalised outreach copy for the event with a LangChain LLM call. - fix: Wrap the prompt and OpenAI call in a LangChain LLMChain (or a small Chain implementation) and show it wired in orchestrator; keep the existing fallback template.
- **19-proactive-outreach-orchestration**: A send agent determines the customer's preferred channel from their data and dispatches the generated copy through that channel (e.g. email, SMS or push). - fix: Provide provider-specific send logic in src/sendAgent.js (guarded behind env vars) or show one concrete provider implementation (e.g., SMTP) and add required env placeholders to .env.example. Also add an explicit idempotency check before sending.
- **19-proactive-outreach-orchestration**: A .env.example lists every setting with placeholder values. - fix: Add missing optional env keys the code reads (e.g., MAX_POOL_SIZE, MIN_POOL_SIZE) and stub placeholders for common send-provider envs (SMTP_HOST, SMTP_USER, TWILIO_SID, etc.) so the example enumerates the settings developers will need to supply.
- **20-identity-resolution-multi-agent**: A match agent built with LangChain retrieves candidate duplicate profiles from Azure DocumentDB through the Beanie models and uses an LLM call to score how similar each candidate is to the profile being resolved, returning the candidates together with their similarity scores. - fix: Wrap match logic in a LangChain agent or call an LLM (configurable via env var) to re-score/augment vector results; e.g. add a LangChain agent that accepts candidate docs and returns LLM-assisted similarity scores.
- **20-identity-resolution-multi-agent**: A merge agent built with LangChain takes the matched profiles and consolidates them into a single golden record (e.g. choosing or combining field values across the matched profiles). - fix: Implement the merge agent as a LangChain agent (or a LangChain chain) that uses the LLM provider configured by env vars; keep the deterministic fallback but expose the LangChain wrapper for the LLM path.
- **20-identity-resolution-multi-agent**: An audit agent built with LangChain records every merge decision in Azure DocumentDB through a Beanie model, and each record carries the confidence score of the merge and the source documents (or references to them) that went into it. - fix: Record audit entries exactly as now, but optionally expose audit_agent as a LangChain tool/agent or document why a LangChain wrapper isn't needed. Ensure audit entries avoid storing raw LLM keys or full request/response bodies.
- **20-identity-resolution-multi-agent**: The vector index dimensions match the embedding model's output. - fix: Either derive DIM from the chosen embedding model (if possible) or document in README and assert at startup that embedding vectors returned by the provider have the configured dimensionality; add a runtime check before index creation.
- **20-store-locator-with-geospatial-search**: The README covers what the sample shows, prerequisites, setup, run, and clean-up. - fix: Add a 'Clean-up' section to README describing how to drop the stores collection, remove sample documents, and any cloud cleanup steps (if IaC provided, show `az`/Bicep/Terraform destroy commands).

- **01-fraud-detection-with-vector-similarity**: The vector index dimensions match the embedding model's output. - fix: Create the index from code in seed.py (use len(vec) for dimensions) and document the match; if you keep MCP-based verification, add a fallback createIndexes path in code.

- **02-clinical-trial-matching-orchestration**: The vector index dimensions match the embedding model's output. - fix: Use EMBEDDING_DIM when creating the service-side index (createIndexes runCommand) and validate that the index 'dimensions' equals EMBEDDING_DIM; fail early with a clear error if a mismatch would be created.

- **03-next-best-action-multi-agent**: The vector index dimensions match the embedding model's output. - fix: Either (a) fix the MCP tool invocation (diagnose MCP server failure) so the sample creates the cosmosSearch vector index with dimensions matching EMBEDDING_DIMS, or (b) add a clear script/README snippet showing the exact createIndex command to run manually (include EMBEDDING_DIMS placeholder).

- **04-earnings-call-rag-assistant**: Provide a single-agent question-answering flow that takes an analyst's natural-language question, embeds it and runs a $search / cosmosSearch vector similarity query against Azure DocumentDB that returns the top-k most relevant chunks with their text and metadata. - fix: Add an aggregation path that issues db.collection.aggregate([{ $search: { cosmosSearch: { vector: <queryVec>, path: 'embedding', k: <k> } } }, { $project: { score: { $meta: 'searchScore' }, text:1, source:1, start_time:1 } }]) when a vector index exists; keep the client-side fallback for environments without a vector index.
- **05-branch-performance-analytics**: A LangChain tool (Python LangChain) takes an analyst's natural-language question and queries the cached summary documents in Azure DocumentDB to answer it. - fix: Add langchain to requirements and ensure CI/installation uses a pydantic-core wheel or platform where pydantic-core builds succeed (or provide an optional adapter module). - **05-branch-performance-analytics**: The tool is attached to a LangChain agent or chain so that an analyst can submit a natural-language question (e.g. through a FastAPI endpoint) and receive a natural-language answer derived from the cached summaries. - fix: If the requirement strictly requires LangChain, add langchain to requirements and refactor /ask to construct a LangChain Agent or Chain that wraps the existing retrieval function; otherwise document clearly in README that the endpoint implements a LangChain-style Tool and how to migrate to LangChain.
- **08-regulatory-compliance-rag-checker**: Create a cosmosSearch vector index (kind vector-ivf, vector-hnsw or vector-diskann) on the embedding field of each collection in code, with dimensions matching the embedding model's output. - fix: Add a clear, runnable 'create_indexes_admin.py' that uses pymongo runCommand with example cosmosSearchOptions, and document in README that operators must run it with admin privileges (or set ENABLE_MANAGEMENT_TOOLS=true for the MCP tool) so the index is created and can be verified.

- **20-provider-directory-semantic-search**: Embed the incoming request with LlamaIndex and run the similarity search inside Azure DocumentDB with a $search / cosmosSearch query against that index using the request embedding. - fix: Either add LlamaIndex to the runtime (requirements) and use its embedding adapter in main.py/seed.py, or update README to state explicitly that LlamaIndex is optional and show the minimal code changes to enable it (preferred: add a small helper that delegates to LlamaIndex when installed).

### skill_guidance_not_followed (20)

- **02-ai-powered-product-recommendations**: Use the Azure DocumentDB skill to read co-purchase order history from Azure DocumentDB (code shows an explicit call or adapter labeled "Azure DocumentDB skill" that queries the database). - fix: Add a thin adapter module (e.g., src/skill_adapters/documentdb.js) that wraps the DocumentDB skill guidance (or MCP calls) and update README to mention it; or update the skill to provide an explicit adapter template the builder can `require()`.
- **03-substitute-product-finder**: A `/substitute` endpoint accepts a product ID, looks up that product's stored embedding and runs an Azure DocumentDB `$search` / cosmosSearch query with similarity COS that returns the 5 closest alternative products (the requested product itself excluded). - fix: Replace the knnBeta-style $search payload with the DocumentDB `$search` / 'cosmosSearch' payload and specify similarity: 'COS' per the vector-search docs; project $meta 'searchScore' and limit downstream results. (Example: build a cosmosSearch object matching createIndexes 'name' and path 'embedding'.)
- **04-natural-language-product-search**: The hybrid search tool runs a full-text search over the product catalog in Azure DocumentDB using a search index created in code with createSearchIndexes and queried with the $search aggregation stage through a Python MongoDB driver, and keeps the ranked results it returns. - fix: Add a safe index-creation step (e.g., a script or seed flag that calls db.runCommand({createSearchIndexes: ...}) using the index JSON) or an explicit shell snippet that runs the template with mongosh during setup.
- **04-natural-language-product-search**: The same tool embeds the rewritten intent and runs a vector search against Azure DocumentDB with $search / cosmosSearch over the embedding field stored on each product document, so the similarity ranking is computed by the database, and keeps those ranked results. - fix: Provide and run a safe createIndexes/createSearchIndexes command (script or seeded step) that applies indexes in indexes/vector_index.json (or call the MCP create index tool with the correct payload).
- **04-natural-language-product-search**: A cosmosSearch vector index (kind vector-ivf, vector-hnsw or vector-diskann) is created in code on the product documents' embedding field, with dimensions matching the embedding model the app uses. - fix: Either add a guarded index-creation script that runs db.runCommand(createIndexes/createSearchIndexes) using the templates, or provide an opt-in CLI flag in seed/seed.py (e.g., --create-indexes) that applies the templates via pymongo's db.command call.
- **04-natural-language-product-search**: The vector index parameters are chosen for the sample's data size, and the README says how to change them for a larger one. - fix: Add a short 'Index tuning' section in indexes/README.md describing tradeoffs and sample parameter values for small (<=10k), medium (10k-50k) and large (>50k) collections, and an example command to recreate the index with new params.
- **04-natural-language-product-search**: A seed script loads sample data and creates the index, and can be run twice without duplicating data. - fix: Add an opt-in --create-indexes flag to seed/seed.py that applies the index templates via db.command or separate script, guarded so it only runs when the user explicitly opts in.
- **06-symptom-triage-chatbot-rag**: The vector index parameters are chosen for the sample's data size, and the README says how to change them for a larger one. - fix: Add an 'Index tuning' section to README explaining the chosen defaults and how to change maxDegree / lBuild / lSearch / k for larger datasets (one paragraph and example values).
- **06-symptom-triage-chatbot-rag**: Unit tests run without a live cluster. - fix: Provide test fixtures that either (a) monkeypatch get_client() to return a local in-memory or mocked client, or (b) document and include a small script to run the DocumentDB local gateway container in CI and point DOCUMENTDB_URI at it; update tests to use the fixture.
- **07-customer-review-sentiment-pipeline**: One database client is created and reused across requests. - fix: Change db.init_db/get_motor_client to create a module-level AsyncMongo/AsyncMongoClient singleton (one client per process), return that, and add a close/shutdown hook. Minimal prompt: add to starter-skill rule 'Make a single module-level Async client and expose get_motor_client()'.
- **08-interest-rate-scenario-modeler**: A seed script loads sample data and creates the index, and can be run twice without duplicating data. - fix: Move index creation into the seed/migration script (seed.py) and check existing indexes before creating, so seed both loads data and ensures indexes in one idempotent step.
- **09-clinical-notes-de-identification**: Enforce schema and model validation at the application or DB layer (Beanie/Pydantic or MongoDB validation) and include a schemaVersion field to support migrations. - fix: Add a schemaVersion field to both RawNote and DeidNote models and optionally add a small collection validation document or document the migration plan in README.
- **11-correspondent-banking-risk-screener**: The vector index parameters are chosen for the sample's data size, and the README says how to change them for a larger one. - fix: Add recommended default algorithm params (efConstruction, M, numLists / nlist / lBuild etc.) for the chosen index kind, explain the choice for demo-scale data and provide a README section on how to change them for larger datasets.
- **11-correspondent-banking-risk-screener**: Search results include the similarity score. - fix: Project the searchScore via {$project: {score: {$meta: 'searchScore'}, ...}} in the aggregation pipeline and include that 'score' in the matched entries returned by the API.
- **11-preference-center-consent-store**: Recording consent appends a new entry to the consent history sub-array (e.g. with a push update that leaves earlier entries in place) and updates the document's current channel preferences and consentVersion. - fix: Use an atomic single-document update (findOneAndUpdate / updateOne with $push and $set and upsert=true) instead of read-modify-write; e.g. perform find_one_and_update(filter={'customerId': id}, update={'$push': {'history': history_item_dict}, '$set': {'channels': new_channels, 'retention_expires_at': retention, 'consentVersion': new_version}}, upsert=True, return_document=ReturnDocument.AFTER).
- **11-preference-center-consent-store**: Withdrawing consent appends a new entry to the consent history sub-array (e.g. with a push update that leaves earlier entries in place) and updates the document's current channel preferences. - fix: Same as for recording: implement the withdraw as an atomic find_one_and_update with $push (history) and $set (channels, retention_expires_at, consentVersion) to avoid race conditions.
- **19-cross-sell-recommendation-beanie**: A cosmosSearch vector index of kind vector-ivf, vector-hnsw or vector-diskann is created in code on the pair documents' embedding field with dimensions matching the OpenAI embedding model used and a similarity of COS, L2 or IP. - fix: Add an idempotent index-creation script (e.g., index_create.py or a `--create-index` flag in seed.py) that calls the DocumentDB `createSearchIndexes` path (driver runCommand or MCP) using EMBEDDING_DIMENSIONS and chosen kind (e.g., `vector-diskann`) and documents the command in README.
- **19-cross-sell-recommendation-beanie**: The vector index dimensions match the embedding model's output. - fix: Add an idempotent index-creation script that reads EMBEDDING_DIMENSIONS and sets the index `dimensions` accordingly; include a runtime check that stored vectors' length equals EMBEDDING_DIMENSIONS before creating the index.
- **19-proactive-outreach-orchestration**: The vector index dimensions match the embedding model's output. - fix: Add an idempotent index-creation script that uses the DocumentDB createIndexes shape with cosmosSearchOptions and reads EMBEDDING_MODEL (or EMBEDDING_DIMS) from env to set dimensions.
- **19-proactive-outreach-orchestration**: The vector index parameters are chosen for the sample's data size, and the README says how to change them for a larger one. - fix: Ship a documented default for vector-index parameters in README and an index-creation script that exposes build/search parameters (lBuild/lSearch/maxDegree/numLists) via env vars or a small config file.
