- **20-identity-resolution-multi-agent** D014: I chose pymongo>=4.5, motor>=3.1 and beanie>=1.12 (modern drivers compatible with MongoDB 7.0). - These versions are compatible with MongoDB 7.0 wire protocol and Beanie async usage; they align with the environment and installed requirements.
- **20-identity-resolution-multi-agent** D016: I chose OpenAI for embeddings and LLM: embeddings model 'text-embedding-3-small' (1536 dims) and LLM 'gpt-3.5-turbo' as the default. The code falls back to synthetic embeddings when OPENAI_API_KEY is not set. - OpenAI is a common default, and the sample must allow running without keys; fallback synthetic embeddings enable offline runs and verification.
- **20-identity-resolution-multi-agent** D019: I chose the data model and collections named 'profiles', 'golden_profiles', and 'merge_audits' (Profile, GoldenRecord, AuditEntry Beanie models). - Matches the prompt and keeps the sample focused on identity-resolution artifacts; models are already implemented in src/models.py.
- **20-interest-accrual-batch-processor** D002: I chose Mongoose 7.x (ODM) for the sample, pinned with a caret range in package.json. - Mongoose provides convenient schemas, validation, and an easy-to-understand model layer for a short sample; it aligns with the prompt review assumption and works well for cursor + bulkWrite patterns.
- **20-interest-accrual-batch-processor** D004: I chose node-cron with default schedule '0 0 * * *' (midnight UTC) and provide a --once CLI flag for manual runs. - node-cron is lightweight and well-known; midnight UTC is an unambiguous default for a daily accrual job; --once supports manual verification runs.
- **20-interest-accrual-batch-processor** D005: I chose a default batch size of 500 for cursor pagination and bulk writes. - 500 provides a balance between memory usage and throughput for a sample; it's adjustable via env var BATCH_SIZE for production tuning.
- **20-open-banking-api-cache-layer** D007: I chose to implement both an Express-compatible middleware and an exported wrapper function that can be used around an HTTP client (axios). - Providing both surfaces makes the sample broadly useful: middleware for routing scenarios and wrapper for programmatic outbound calls; both are useful for open banking caching patterns.
- **20-open-banking-api-cache-layer** D008: I chose demo-friendly defaults: TTL = 5 seconds and stale-while-revalidate window = 10 seconds for the included demo; the library supports configurable TTLs for production use. - Short TTLs let the one-shot demo show fresh, stale, and background refresh behavior without long waits; production usage should set longer values and is configurable.
- **20-provider-directory-semantic-search** D006: I chose not to shard the sample collection (single-shard, no shard key) - Sharding is unnecessary for a small demo; avoids complexity in sample and cluster operations
- **20-store-locator-with-geospatial-search** D003: I chose to use Mongoose ^7.0.0 (latest 7.x) in package.json - Keeps compatibility with Node 24 and allows receiving minor fixes while staying in Mongoose 7 major version.
- **20-store-locator-with-geospatial-search** D006: I chose to keep the stores collection unsharded for this sample - Sharding introduces complexity and requires cluster changes; for this sample the focus is geo queries and 2dsphere index behaviour, not scaling across shards.
- **20-store-locator-with-geospatial-search** D013: I chose to accept lat, lon and radiusMeters as query parameters (e.g., /nearby?lat=...&lon=...&radiusMeters=...) and document this in the README. - Query parameters make the endpoint easy to call in browsers and curl; it's a common pattern for simple read-only queries and matches the sample's focus on demonstration rather than complex request bodies.
- **retail-semantic-search** D002: I chose default pool sizing, maxIdleTimeMS=120000, and 10-second connect/server-selection timeouts for this sequential local CLI. - Sequential ingestion and one interactive request at a time do not need pool tuning; bounded startup enables a useful failure message without imposing a short limit on index builds.
